   414	    # (consensus + edges from reads only). OFF => byte-identical.
   415	    denovo_graph: bool = False
   416	    denovo_graph_tol: int = 6
   417	    denovo_graph_min_edge_reads: int = 2
   418	    # 5'-TSS brake for --denovo-graph. dRNA truncation is 5'-ward, so a truncated
   419	    # read of a LONG transcript stops at a random 5' position while a COMPLETE read
   420	    # of a genuine short isoform stops at that isoform's real TSS. A chain whose
   421	    # 5'-terminal junction sits at a TSS peak (>= tss_frac of its reads pile their
   422	    # 5'-end within tss_tol bp, and >= tss_min_reads reads) is NOT extended 5'-ward,
   423	    # so short isoforms contained inside a longer transcript survive instead of
   424	    # being merged away. Validated on p00: real short-isoform TSS carry 40-90% of a
   425	    # locus's read-5'-ends; degradation-only starts scatter and never cross frac.
   426	    denovo_graph_tss_brake: bool = True
   427	    denovo_graph_tss_tol: int = 20
   428	    denovo_graph_tss_min_reads: int = 3
   429	    denovo_graph_tss_frac: float = 0.4
   430	    # The mirror of Lever 2 for GTF-passthrough (source="gtf") candidates: drop a
   431	    # guided multi-exon candidate if ANY of its junctions is spliced by fewer than
   432	    # guided_junction_min_reads directly-observed reads (primary-read CIGAR introns,
   433	    # strand-keyed, within guided_junction_reads_tol bp of BOTH boundaries). Novel
   434	    # candidates keep going through Lever 2; fusion/mono are exempt.
   435	    # WHY: Lever 2 + the coordinate-EXACT gates are novel-only, so a jitter-corrupted

     1	"""
     2	Interval Manager for generating isolated genomic intervals with strand separation
     3	
     4	This module provides functions to generate non-overlapping genomic intervals
     5	by analyzing both GTF annotation and BAM read alignments. Fusion reads are
     6	identified and skipped during interval generation.
     7	
     8	Key features:
     9	- Strand-separated intervals (no mixing of + and -)
    10	- No gene/transcript IDs stored in intervals
    11	- Read counts tracked per interval
    12	- Only essential info: chrom, start, end, strand, read_count
    13	"""
    14	
    15	from typing import List, Dict, Tuple, Optional, Set, Any, Union
    16	from pathlib import Path
    17	from collections import defaultdict
    18	from dataclasses import dataclass,field
    19	
    20	
    21	from .io_bam import BamReader
    22	from .io_gtf import GTFReader
    23	from ..utils.log_config import get_package_logger
    24	
    25	logger = get_package_logger(__name__)
    26	
    27	
    28	@dataclass
    29	class GenomicInterval:
    30	    """Non-overlapping genomic interval with strand separation"""
    31	    chrom: str
    32	    start: int  # 0-based
    33	    end: int
    34	    strand: Optional[str] = None  # '+' or '-', None for unstranded
    35	    read_count: int = 0  # Number of reads mapped to this interval
    36	    interval_id: Optional[str] = None
    37	    attrs: Optional[List] = field(default_factory=list)
    38	    three_prime_pos: Optional[List] = field(default_factory=list)
    39	
    40	    @property
    41	    def interval_tuple(self) -> Tuple[str, int, int]:
    42	        """Get interval as (chrom, start, end) tuple"""
    43	        return (self.chrom, self.start, self.end)
    44	
    45	    @property
    46	    def region_string(self) -> str:
    47	        """Get region string for BAM/GTF extraction"""
    48	        return f"{self.chrom}:{self.start}-{self.end}"
    49	
    50	    def overlaps(self, other: 'GenomicInterval') -> bool:
    51	        """Check if this interval overlaps with another"""
    52	        return (self.chrom == other.chrom and
    53	                self.start < other.end and
    54	                self.end > other.start)
    55	
    56	    def merge(self, other: 'GenomicInterval') -> 'GenomicInterval':
    57	        """Merge with another interval (must overlap AND same strand)"""
    58	        if self.strand != other.strand:
    59	            raise ValueError("Cannot merge intervals on different strands")
    60	
    61	        
    62	        return GenomicInterval(
    63	            chrom=self.chrom,
    64	            start=min(self.start, other.start),
    65	            end=max(self.end, other.end),
    66	            strand=self.strand,
    67	            read_count=self.read_count + other.read_count,
    68	            interval_id=self.interval_id if self.interval_id else other.interval_id,
    69	            attrs=self.attrs + other.attrs,
    70	            three_prime_pos=self.three_prime_pos + other.three_prime_pos
    71	        )
    72	
    73	
    74	def intervals_to_bed(
    75	    intervals: List[GenomicInterval],
    76	    output_path: Union[str, Path, None] = None) -> Optional[str]:
    77	    """
    78	    Export GenomicInterval objects to BED format.
    79	    
    80	    Args:
    81	        intervals: List of GenomicInterval objects
    82	        output_path: Path to output file, file object, or None to return string
    83	    Returns:
    84	        BED formatted string if output_path is None, otherwise None
    85	    """
    86	    if not intervals:
    87	        if output_path is None:
    88	            return ""
    89	        return None
    90	    
    91	    # Sort intervals by chrom, then start position
    92	    bed_lines = []
    93	    
    94	    # Add UCSC BED header if requested
    95	    
    96	    for i, interval in enumerate(intervals):
    97	        # Required BED3 columns: chrom, start, end
    98	        chrom = interval.chrom
    99	        start = interval.start  # Already 0-based
   100	        end = interval.end      # Already exclusive
   101	        
   102	        # Optional columns
   103	        name = interval.interval_id if interval.interval_id else f"interval_{i}"
   104	        
   105	      
   106	        score = interval.read_count if interval.read_count else 0
   107	        
   108	        # Strand handling
   109	        strand = interval.strand if interval.strand else "."
   110	        
   111	        # Build BED fields
   112	        bed_fields = [chrom, str(start), str(end),name, str(score), strand]
   113	    
   114	        
   115	        # Join fields with tabs (BED format requirement)
   116	        bed_line = "\t".join(bed_fields)
   117	        bed_lines.append(bed_line)
   118	    
   119	    bed_content = "\n".join(bed_lines)
   120	    
   121	    # Output to file, file object, or return string
   122	    if output_path is None:
   123	        return bed_content
   124	    elif hasattr(output_path, 'write'):
   125	        # output_path is a file-like object
   126	        output_path.write(bed_content)
   127	        return None
   128	    else:
   129	        # output_path is a string/Path
   130	        with open(output_path, 'w') as f:
   131	            f.write(bed_content)
   132	        return None
   133	
   134	
   135	def is_fusion_read(read_dict: Dict) -> bool:
   136	    """
   137	    Check if a read is a fusion candidate based on soft-clip length
   138	
   139	    Args:
   140	        read_dict: Dictionary representation of the read from BamReader
%                                                                               (base) [36m┌─[30m([32m/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin[30m)[36m────────────────────────────[30m([36mlogan[30m@[32mmnemosyne4:pts/24[30m)[36m─┐
[36m└[34m─([33m16:12:29[34m[00m on [32mdev[00m[36m ?✭[34m)[36m──> [?2004hnl -ba fin/io/interval_manager.py | sed -n '135,210p'[?2004l
   135	def is_fusion_read(read_dict: Dict) -> bool:
   136	    """
   137	    Check if a read is a fusion candidate based on soft-clip length
   138	
   139	    Args:
   140	        read_dict: Dictionary representation of the read from BamReader
   141	
   142	    Returns:
   143	        True if read is a fusion candidate
   144	    """
   145	    
   146	    if read_dict.get('is_supplementary'):
   147	        return True
   148	    
   149	    SOFT_CLIP_THRESHOLD = 250
   150	    # logger.debug(f'Checking Fusion reads:{read_dict.get("cigartuples")}')
   151	    if not read_dict.get('cigartuples'):
   152	        return False
   153	
   154	    # Check for long soft-clips at read ends
   155	    cigartuples = read_dict['cigartuples']
   156	    if not cigartuples:
   157	        return False
   158	
   159	    # Check first and last CIGAR operations for soft-clips
   160	    first_op, first_len = cigartuples[0]
   161	    last_op, last_len = cigartuples[-1]
   162	
   163	    # Check if soft-clips exceed threshold at either end
   164	    if first_op == 4 and first_len >= SOFT_CLIP_THRESHOLD:
   165	        return True
   166	    if last_op == 4 and last_len >= SOFT_CLIP_THRESHOLD:
   167	        return True
   168	    
   169	    
   170	    # OR I can just use read_dict.get('is_supplementary')
   171	    return False
   172	
   173	
   174	def extract_strand_from_read(read_dict: Dict) -> Optional[str]:
   175	    """
   176	    Extract strand information from a read alignment
   177	
   178	    For some BAM files, strand may not be directly available.
   179	    This function can be extended based on specific needs.
   180	
   181	    Args:
   182	        read_dict: Dictionary representation of the read
   183	
   184	    Returns:
   185	        Strand ('+' or '-') or None if not available
   186	    """
   187	    # Placeholder - can be extended based on specific BAM format
   188	    # Some BAM files store strand in flags or tags
   189	    return '+' if read_dict.get('is_forward') else '-'
   190	
   191	def extract_three_prime_pos(read_dict: Dict) -> Optional[int]:
   192	    """Return the 3' genomic coordinate for a read, or None if unmapped.
   193	
   194	    Forward reads -> reference_end (exclusive); reverse reads -> reference_start.
   195	    pysam returns reference_end=None for unmapped reads; we must propagate
   196	    None instead of TypeError'ing inside int().
   197	    """
   198	    pos = (
   199	        read_dict.get('reference_end')
   200	        if read_dict.get('is_forward')
   201	        else read_dict.get('reference_start')
   202	    )
   203	    if pos is None:
   204	        return None
   205	    return int(pos)
   206

[36m└[34m─([33m16:12:17[34m[00m on [32mdev[00m[36m ?✭[34m)[36m──> nl -ba fin/io/io_bam.py | sed -n '1,160p'[?2004l
     1	"""
     2	BAM/SAM file format parser using pysam
     3	"""
     4	
     5	from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Iterator, Tuple, Any, Generator
     6	from pathlib import Path
     7	import logging
     8	
     9	try:
    10	    import pysam
    11	except ImportError:
    12	    raise ImportError(
    13	        "pysam is required for BAM/SAM support. "
    14	        "Install it with: pip install pysam"
    15	    )
    16	
    17	
    18	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19	
    20	
    21	class BamReader:
    22	    """Reader for BAM/SAM alignment files"""
    23	
    24	    def __init__(self, file_path: str, index: bool = True):
    25	        """
    26	        Initialize BAM/SAM reader
    27	
    28	        Args:
    29	            file_path: Path to the BAM/SAM file
    30	            index: Whether to load the index for random access
    31	        """
    32	        self.file_path = Path(file_path)
    33	        if not self.file_path.exists():
    34	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"BAM/SAM file not found: {file_path}")
    35	
    36	        self._alignment_file = None
    37	        self._index = index
    38	        self._is_open = False
    39	
    40	    def __enter__(self):
    41	        """Context manager entry"""
    42	        self.open()
    43	        return self
    44	
    45	    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
    46	        """Context manager exit"""
    47	        self.close()
    48	
    49	    def open(self):
    50	        """Open the alignment file"""
    51	        try:
    52	            mode = 'rb' if self.file_path.suffix.lower() == '.bam' else 'r'
    53	            self._alignment_file = pysam.AlignmentFile(str(self.file_path), mode, index_filename=str(self.file_path) + '.bai' if self._index else None)
    54	            self._is_open = True
    55	
    56	            logger.info(f"Opened alignment file: {self.file_path}")
    57	        except Exception as e:
    58	            logger.error(f"Failed to open alignment file {self.file_path}: {e}")
    59	            raise
    60	
    61	    def close(self):
    62	        """Close the alignment file"""
    63	        if self._alignment_file is not None:
    64	            self._alignment_file.close()
    65	            self._alignment_file = None
    66	            self._is_open = False
    67	            logger.info(f"Closed alignment file: {self.file_path}")
    68	
    69	    @property
    70	    def is_open(self) -> bool:
    71	        """Check if file is open"""
    72	        return self._is_open
    73	
    74	    @property
    75	    def header(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    76	        """
    77	        Get file header
            Dictionary containing header information
    81	        """
    82	        if self._alignment_file is None:
    83	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
    84	        return dict(self._alignment_file.header)
    85	
    86	    @property
    87	    def references(self) -> List[Tuple[str, int]]:
    88	        """
    89	        Get list of reference sequences
    90	
    91	        Returns:
    92	            List of tuples (reference_name, reference_length)
    93	        """
    94	        if self._alignment_file is None:
    95	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
    96	
    97	        return [(self._alignment_file.get_reference_name(i), self._alignment_file.get_reference_length(i))
    98	                for i in range(self._alignment_file.nreferences)]
    99	
   100	    def count(self, region: Optional[str] = None, read_callback: str = 'all') -> int:
   101	        """
   102	        Count alignments
   103	
   104	        Args:
   105	            region: Region to count (e.g., 'chr1:1000-2000')
   106	            read_callback: Read filter (all, mapped, unmapped)
   107	
   108	        Returns:
   109	            Number of alignments
   110	        """
   111	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   112	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   113	
   114	        try:
   115	            return self._alignment_file.count(region=region, read_callback=read_callback)
   116	        except Exception as e:
   117	            logger.error(f"Failed to count alignments: {e}")
   118	            return 0
   119	
   120	    def fetch(self, reference: Optional[str] = None, start: Optional[int] = None,
   121	              end: Optional[int] = None, region: Optional[str] = None,
   122	              until_eof: bool = False) -> Iterator[pysam.AlignedSegment]:
   123	        """
   124	        Fetch alignments from a region
   125	
   126	        Args:
   127	            reference: Reference name
   128	            start: Start position (0-based)
   129	            end: End position
   130	            region: Region string (e.g., 'chr1:1000-2000')
   131	            until_eof: Continue to end of file
   132	
   133	        Returns:
   134	            Iterator over AlignedSegment objects
   135	        """
   136	        if self._alignment_file is None:
   137	            raise RuntimeError("Alignment file not opened. Call open() first.")
   138	
   139	        try:
   140	            # Pass by keyword: pysam.fetch's 5th positional parameter is `tid`,
   141	            # not `until_eof`. Passing until_eof positionally lands it in the tid
   142	            # slot, and tid=False (==0) overrides `contig`, so every region fetch
   143	            # silently returns reads from reference 0 regardless of `reference`.
   144	            return self._alignment_file.fetch(
   145	                contig=reference, start=start, stop=end,
   146	                region=region, until_eof=until_eof,
   147	            )
   148	        except Exception as e:
   149	            logger.error(f"Failed to fetch alignments: {e}")
   150	            return iter([])
   151	
   152	    def get_read(self, read_id: str) -> Optional[pysam.AlignedSegment]:
   153	        """
   154	        Get a specific read by name (requires indexed BAM)
   155	
   156	        Args:
   157	            read_id: Read name/ID
   158	
   159	        Returns:
   160	            AlignedSegment object or None if not found
%                                                                               (base) [36m┌─[30m([32m/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin[30m)[36m────────────────────────────[30m([36mlogan[30m@[32mmnemosyne4:pts/25[30m)[36m─┐
[36m└[34m─([33m16:12:31[34m[00m on [32mdev[00m[36m ?✭[34m)[36m──> [?2004hnl -ba fin/io/io_bam.py | sed -n '190,245p'[?2004l
   190	            return None
   191	
   192	    def get_reference_sequence(self, alignment:pysam.AlignedSegment) -> str:
   193	        if alignment.is_secondary:
   194	            return None
   195	        try:
   196	            forward_ref_seq = alignment.get_reference_sequence().upper()
   197	        except ValueError:
   198	            # MD tag not present in BAM (minimap2 without --MD).
   199	            # Downstream consumers default to "" so we can return None safely.
   200	            return None
   201	        return forward_ref_seq if not alignment.is_reverse else self.reverse_complement(forward_ref_seq)
   202	    
   203	    def alignment_to_dict(self, alignment: pysam.AlignedSegment) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   204	        """
   205	        Convert AlignedSegment to dictionary
   206	
   207	        Args:
   208	            alignment: AlignedSegment object
   209	
   210	        Returns:
   211	            Dictionary representation
   212	        """
   213	        if alignment is None:
   214	            return {}
   215	
   216	        return {
   217	            'query_name': alignment.query_name,
   218	            'flag': alignment.flag,
   219	            'is_reverse': alignment.is_reverse,
   220	            'is_secondary': alignment.is_secondary,
   221	            'is_duplicate': alignment.is_duplicate,
   222	            'is_supplementary': alignment.is_supplementary,
   223	            'cigartuples': alignment.cigartuples,
   224	            'reference_name': alignment.reference_name,
   225	            'reference_id': alignment.reference_id,
   226	            'reference_start': alignment.reference_start,
   227	            'reference_end': alignment.reference_end,
   228	            'query_alignment_start': alignment.query_alignment_start,
   229	            'query_alignment_end': alignment.query_alignment_end,
   230	            'query_alignment_length': alignment.query_alignment_length,
   231	            'mapping_quality': alignment.mapping_quality,
   232	            'template_length': alignment.template_length,
   233	            'reference_sequence': self.get_reference_sequence(alignment),
   234	            'is_forward': alignment.is_forward,
   235	            'is_mapped': not alignment.is_unmapped,
   236	            'query_length': alignment.query_length,
   237	            'query_sequence': alignment.query_sequence,
   238	        }
   239	
   240	    def get_reads_in_region(self, region: str, max_reads: Optional[int] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   241	        """
   242	        Get all reads in a specific region
   243	
   244	        Args:
   245	            region: Region string (e.g., 'chr1:1000-2000')
%                                                                               (base) [36m┌─[30m([32m/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin[30m)[36m────────────────────────────[30m([36mlogan[30m@[32mmnemosyne4:pts/25[30m)[36m─┐

Real isoforms deposit ~1 soft mass per hard read (ratio ~1); wobble shadows borrow fractional soft crumbs from a high-abundance structural near-copy and show an inflated ratio — catches the high-relative-abundance shadows the fraction/cluster-recheck levers miss.      1	"""Unit tests for generation-side chain clustering (chain_cluster).
     2	
     3	Group reads by EXACT chain (3' ignored); fold EXACT sub-chains into their container;
     4	union-find the survivors into clusters by wobble / cassette / containment. Members
     5	(distinct candidates) are kept; no snap, no shadow generation.
     6	"""
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	from fin.candidates.dataclasses import IntronChain
    10	from fin.candidates.chain_cluster import (
    11	    cluster_read_chains, _exact_subchain, _wobble, _contains, _cassette, _related,
    12	)
    13	
    14	J1, J2, J3 = (100, 200), (300, 400), (500, 600)
    15	
    16	
    17	def _rc(pairs):
    18	    return [({"query_name": rid}, IntronChain(introns=introns)) for rid, introns in pairs]
    19	
    20	
    21	def _member_chains(cluster):
    22	    return {m.chain.introns for m in cluster.members}
    23	
    24	
    25	class TestPredicates:
    26	    def test_exact_subchain(self):
    27	        assert _exact_subchain((J2, J3), (J1, J2, J3))
    28	        assert not _exact_subchain(((302, 398), J3), (J1, J2, J3))  # wobbled -> not exact
    29	        assert not _exact_subchain((J1, J2, J3), (J1, J2, J3))       # equal length
    30	
    31	    def test_wobble(self):
    32	        assert _wobble((J1, J2), ((102, 198), (301, 402)), bp=6)
    33	        assert not _wobble((J1, J2), (J1, J2, J3), bp=6)
    34	
    35	    def test_contains_tolerant(self):
    36	        assert _contains(((302, 398), J3), (J1, J2, J3), bp=6)   # wobbled suffix
    37	        assert not _contains(((330, 370), J3), (J1, J2, J3), bp=6)
    38	
    39	    def test_cassette(self):
    40	        long_ = (J1, (300, 350), (370, 600))    # small exon 350..370
    41	        short = (J1, (300, 600))
    42	        assert _cassette(long_, short, bp=6, max_exon_bp=70)
    43	        assert not _cassette(long_, short, bp=6, max_exon_bp=10)
    44	
    45	
    46	class TestClustering:
    47	    def test_low_support_exact_subchain_collapses(self):
    48	        # exact suffix with FEW reads (1) vs a well-supported container (4 reads):
    49	        # folded away (a truncation shadow).
    50	        cl = cluster_read_chains(_rc(
    51	            [("a1", (J1, J2, J3)), ("a2", (J1, J2, J3)), ("a3", (J1, J2, J3)),
    52	             ("a4", (J1, J2, J3)), ("b", (J2, J3))]))
    53	        assert len(cl) == 1
    54	        assert _member_chains(cl[0]) == {(J1, J2, J3)}   # only the container survives
    55	        assert cl[0].read_ids == {"a1", "a2", "a3", "a4", "b"}
    56	
    57	    def test_exact_subchain_always_folds_regardless_of_support(self):
    58	        # UNCONDITIONAL fold: an exact contiguous sub-chain is folded into its
    59	        # container even when well-supported. Whether it is a genuine short isoform
    60	        # is NOT decided here -- that is a separate downstream recovery step.
    61	        cl = cluster_read_chains(_rc(
    62	            [("a1", (J1, J2, J3)), ("a2", (J1, J2, J3)),
    63	             ("b1", (J2, J3)), ("b2", (J2, J3))]))
    64	        assert len(cl) == 1
    65	        assert _member_chains(cl[0]) == {(J1, J2, J3)}   # sub-chain folded away
    66	        assert cl[0].read_ids == {"a1", "a2", "b1", "b2"}  # its reads pooled in
    67	
    68	    def test_folded_subchain_retained_as_shadow(self):
    69	        # The folded sub-chain is kept as a SHADOW (provenance) on its container:
    70	        # its own chain + its own reads, for the downstream recovery step.
    71	        cl = cluster_read_chains(_rc(
    72	            [("a1", (J1, J2, J3)), ("a2", (J1, J2, J3)),
    73	             ("b1", (J2, J3)), ("b2", (J2, J3))]))
    74	        (m,) = cl[0].members
    75	        assert m.chain.introns == (J1, J2, J3)
    76	        assert len(m.folded) == 1
    77	        assert m.folded[0].chain.introns == (J2, J3)
    78	        assert m.folded[0].read_ids == {"b1", "b2"}      # shadow's own reads
    79	        assert m.read_ids == {"a1", "a2", "b1", "b2"}    # container still pools all
    80	
    81	    def test_wobble_variants_same_cluster_kept(self):
    82	        # 2bp-wobbled sibling: NOT an exact subchain -> both KEPT, one cluster.
    83	        cl = cluster_read_chains(
    84	            _rc([("a", (J1, J2, J3)), ("b", (J1, (302, 398), J3))]))
    85	        assert len(cl) == 1
    86	        assert _member_chains(cl[0]) == {(J1, J2, J3), (J1, (302, 398), J3)}
    87	        assert cl[0].read_ids == {"a", "b"}
    88	
    89	    def test_cassette_same_cluster_kept(self):
    90	        long_ = (J1, (300, 350), (370, 600))
    91	        short = (J1, (300, 600))
    92	        cl = cluster_read_chains(_rc([("a", long_), ("b", short)]))
    93	        assert len(cl) == 1
    94	        assert _member_chains(cl[0]) == {long_, short}   # both kept
    95	
    96	    def test_tolerant_containment_same_cluster_kept(self):
    97	        # a wobbled (non-exact) sub-chain -> clustered but KEPT (not collapsed).
    98	        cl = cluster_read_chains(
    99	            _rc([("a", (J1, J2, J3)), ("b", ((302, 398), J3))]))
   100	        assert len(cl) == 1
   101	        assert _member_chains(cl[0]) == {(J1, J2, J3), ((302, 398), J3)}
   102	
   103	    def test_distinct_chains_separate(self):
   104	        far = ((1000, 1100), (1300, 1400))
   105	        cl = cluster_read_chains(_rc([("a", (J1, J2, J3)), ("b", far)]))
   106	        assert len(cl) == 2
   107	
   108	    def test_three_prime_ignored_same_chain_pools(self):
   109	        cl = cluster_read_chains(
   110	            _rc([("a", (J1, J2)), ("b", (J1, J2)), ("c", (J1, J2))]))
   111	        assert len(cl) == 1
   112	        assert len(cl[0].members) == 1
   113	        assert cl[0].read_ids == {"a", "b", "c"}
   114	
   115	    def test_mono_exon_singleton(self):
   116	        cl = cluster_read_chains(_rc([("a", ()), ("b", ()), ("m", (J1, J2))]))
   117	        by = {tuple(c.members[0].chain.introns): c for c in cl}
   118	        assert by[()].read_ids == {"a", "b"}
   119	        assert by[(J1, J2)].read_ids == {"m"}
   120	        assert len(cl) == 2
   121	
   122	    def test_representative_is_longest(self):
   123	        # tolerant-containment cluster: 3-intron chain vs a wobbled 2-intron sub-chain
   124	        # (kept, not collapsed) -> representative is the longer (3-intron) member.
   125	        cl = cluster_read_chains(
   126	            _rc([("a", (J1, J2, J3)), ("b", ((302, 398), J3))]))
   127	        assert cl[0].representative.chain.introns == (J1, J2, J3)
   128	
   129	
   130	def _rcs(entries):
   131	    """entries: (rid, introns, ref_start, ref_end) -> read_chains with spans."""
   132	    return [({"query_name": rid, "reference_start": s, "reference_end": e},
   133	             IntronChain(introns=introns)) for rid, introns, s, e in entries]
   134	
   135	
   136	class TestMonoexonFold:
   137	    """fold_monoexon_contained: single-exon reads inside a multi-exon candidate's exon
   138	    are folded into it; intronic / uncontained / junction-crossing mono reads stay."""
   139	
   140	    # multi candidate (J1,J2): exons [50,100) [200,300) [400,500); introns [100,200) [300,400)
   141	    def _rc(self, mono):
   142	        return _rcs([("m1", (J1, J2), 50, 500), ("m2", (J1, J2), 50, 500)] + mono)
   143	
   144	    def test_monoexon_in_exon_folds(self):
   145	        cl = cluster_read_chains(self._rc([("s1", (), 210, 290)]),
   146	                                 fold_monoexon_contained=True)
   147	        assert len(cl) == 1                                   # no standalone mono
   148	        assert _member_chains(cl[0]) == {(J1, J2)}
   149	        assert "s1" in cl[0].read_ids                         # folded into the multi
   150	
   151	    def test_monoexon_in_intron_stays(self):
   152	        cl = cluster_read_chains(self._rc([("s1", (), 120, 180)]),  # inside intron J1
   153	                                 fold_monoexon_contained=True)
   154	        assert any(m.chain.introns == () for c in cl for m in c.members)
   155	
   156	    def test_monoexon_outside_span_stays(self):
   157	        cl = cluster_read_chains(self._rc([("s1", (), 600, 700)]),
   158	                                 fold_monoexon_contained=True)
   159	        assert any(m.chain.introns == () for c in cl for m in c.members)
   160	
   161	    def test_monoexon_crossing_junction_stays(self):
   162	        cl = cluster_read_chains(self._rc([("s1", (), 250, 350)]),  # spans intron J2
   163	                                 fold_monoexon_contained=True)
   164	        assert any(m.chain.introns == () for c in cl for m in c.members)
   165	
   166	    def test_default_off_emits_standalone_mono(self):
   167	        cl = cluster_read_chains(self._rc([("s1", (), 210, 290)]))  # flag off
   168	        assert any(m.chain.introns == () for c in cl for m in c.members)
   169	
   170	
   171	class TestFoldSpanGuard:
   172	    """fold_span_guard: a read whose span runs exonically across a container's EXTRA
   173	    intron (retained-intron / alt isoform) is kept as its own candidate, not folded."""
   174	
   175	    # container (J1,J2,J3): extra intron vs (J1,J2) is J3=(500,600).
   176	    def _rc(self):
   177	        return _rcs([
   178	            ("big1", (J1, J2, J3), 50, 650),
   179	            ("big2", (J1, J2, J3), 50, 650),
   180	            ("trunc", (J1, J2), 50, 450),     # ends before J3 -> truncation (fold)
   181	            ("retain", (J1, J2), 50, 650),    # spans across J3 -> retained (keep)
   182	        ])
   183	
   184	    def test_guard_keeps_retained_intron_folds_truncation(self):
   185	        cl = cluster_read_chains(self._rc(), fold_span_guard=True)
   186	        members = {m.chain.introns: m for c in cl for m in c.members}
   187	        assert (J1, J2, J3) in members
   188	        assert (J1, J2) in members                          # retained-intron kept
   189	        assert "retain" in members[(J1, J2)].read_ids
   190	        assert "trunc" in members[(J1, J2, J3)].read_ids     # truncation folded in
   191	        assert "retain" not in members[(J1, J2, J3)].read_ids
   192	
   193	    def test_default_off_folds_everything(self):
   194	        cl = cluster_read_chains(self._rc())                # guard off (default)
   195	        members = {m.chain.introns for c in cl for m in c.members}
   196	        assert (J1, J2) not in members                       # unconditional fold
   197	        assert (J1, J2, J3) in members

[36m└[34m─([33m16:13:39[34m[00m on [32mdev[00m[36m ?✭[34m)[36m──> nl -ba tests/unit/test_discovery_no_gtf.py[?2004l
     1	"""Tests for novel candidate discovery without a reference GTF (de novo mode).
     2	
     3	When the user runs pyfin without --gtf, discovery must:
     4	1. Produce novel candidates with **non-empty spliced sequences** stitched from
     5	   the genome FASTA + CIGAR-derived intron chain.
     6	2. Apply reverse-complement on the '-' strand.
     7	3. Skip groups where genome bounds are unusable instead of emitting empty
     8	   candidates that f5c will silently drop.
     9	"""
    10	
    11	from __future__ import annotations
    12	
    13	import os
    14	
    15	import pysam
    16	
    17	from fin.candidates.dataclasses import IntronChain
    18	from fin.candidates.discovery import (
    19	    _build_spliced_sequence,
    20	    _exons_from_chain,
    21	    _reverse_complement,
    22	    discover_candidates,
    23	)
    24	from fin.io.interval_manager import GenomicInterval
    25	
    26	
    27	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28	# Helpers for the BAM fixture
    29	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    30	
    31	
    32	def _write_bam_with_spliced_reads(tmp_path: str, chrom: str, chrom_len: int,
    33	                                   reads: list[dict]) -> str:
    34	    """Write a tiny BAM with the given reads (cigar, pos, strand)."""
    35	    header = pysam.AlignmentHeader.from_dict(
    36	        {
    37	            "HD": {"VN": "1.6", "SO": "coordinate"},
    38	            "SQ": [{"SN": chrom, "LN": chrom_len}],
    39	        }
    40	    )
    41	    bam_path = os.path.join(tmp_path, "spliced.bam")
    42	    with pysam.AlignmentFile(bam_path, "wb", header=header) as bam:
    43	        for r in reads:
    44	            seg = pysam.AlignedSegment(header)
    45	            seg.query_name = r["name"]
    46	            # Build a query sequence whose length matches the sum of M ops.
    47	            mlen = sum(length for op, length in r["cigar"] if op in (0, 1, 4, 7, 8))
    48	            seg.query_sequence = "A" * mlen
    49	            seg.flag = 0x10 if r.get("reverse", False) else 0
    50	            seg.reference_id = 0
    51	            seg.reference_start = r["pos"]
    52	            seg.mapping_quality = 60
    53	            seg.cigar = r["cigar"]
    54	            seg.query_qualities = pysam.qualitystring_to_array("I" * mlen)
    55	            bam.write(seg)
    56	    sorted_path = bam_path + ".sorted.bam"
    57	    pysam.sort("-o", sorted_path, bam_path)
    58	    pysam.index(sorted_path)
    59	    return sorted_path
    60

    67	class TestExonsFromChain:
    68	    def test_single_exon(self):
    69	        ex = _exons_from_chain(100, 200, IntronChain(introns=()))
    70	        assert ex == [(100, 200)]
    71	
    72	    def test_two_exons_one_intron(self):
    73	        chain = IntronChain(introns=((150, 170),))
    74	        assert _exons_from_chain(100, 200, chain) == [(100, 150), (170, 200)]
    75	
    76	    def test_three_exons_two_introns(self):
    77	        chain = IntronChain(introns=((120, 140), (160, 180)))
    78	        assert _exons_from_chain(100, 200, chain) == [
    79	            (100, 120),
    80	            (140, 160),
    81	            (180, 200),
    82	        ]

    85	class TestReverseComplement:
    86	    def test_basic(self):
    87	        assert _reverse_complement("ACGT") == "ACGT"
    88	        assert _reverse_complement("AAAA") == "TTTT"
    89	        assert _reverse_complement("GATTACA") == "TGTAATC"
    90	
    91	    def test_preserves_n(self):
    92	        assert _reverse_complement("ANNT") == "ANNT"

    95	class TestBuildSplicedSequence:
    96	    def test_single_exon_plus_strand(self):
    97	        # 30-base genome, no introns -> exact slice
    98	        genome = "ACGT" * 8  # 32 bases
    99	        seq = _build_spliced_sequence(genome, 4, 12, IntronChain(introns=()), "+")
   100	        assert seq == genome[4:12]
   101	
   102	    def test_spliced_two_exon_plus_strand(self):
   103	        # Build a known-content genome where the intron has different bases
   104	        genome = "A" * 10 + "C" * 10 + "G" * 10 + "T" * 10  # 40 bases
   105	        # Exon1 [5,10) = "AAAAA"; intron [10,20) = "CCCCCCCCCC";
   106	        # Exon2 [20,25) = "GGGGG". Spliced should be "AAAAAGGGGG".
   107	        chain = IntronChain(introns=((10, 20),))
   108	        seq = _build_spliced_sequence(genome, 5, 25, chain, "+")
   109	        assert seq == "AAAAAGGGGG"
   110	        assert "C" not in seq  # intron stripped
   111	
   112	    def test_negative_strand_reverse_complements(self):
   113	        genome = "A" * 10 + "C" * 10 + "G" * 10 + "T" * 10
   114	        chain = IntronChain(introns=((10, 20),))
   115	        # Plus strand spliced = "AAAAAGGGGG" -> RC = "CCCCCTTTTT"
   116	        seq = _build_spliced_sequence(genome, 5, 25, chain, "-")
   117	        assert seq == "CCCCCTTTTT"
   118	
   119	    def test_empty_genome_returns_empty(self):
   120	        assert _build_spliced_sequence("", 0, 100, IntronChain(introns=()), "+") == ""
   121	
   122	    def test_clips_to_chromosome_bounds(self):
   123	        # End beyond chromosome should not crash; just clip.
   124	        genome = "ACGT" * 5  # 20 bases
   125	        seq = _build_spliced_sequence(genome, 10, 100, IntronChain(introns=()), "+")
   126	        assert seq == genome[10:]  # clipped at chrom end
   127

   133	
   134	class TestDiscoverCandidatesNoGTF:
   135	    def test_novel_candidates_have_nonempty_sequences(self, tmp_path):
   136	        """gtf_reader=None: every novel candidate must carry a non-empty,
   137	        correctly spliced sequence assembled from the genome FASTA."""
   138	        # Genome with 4 distinct base regions for easy verification:
   139	        # [0,100)=A, [100,200)=C, [200,300)=G, [300,400)=T
   140	        genome = "A" * 100 + "C" * 100 + "G" * 100 + "T" * 100
   141	
   142	        # Three reads with the SAME spliced structure: exon[20,100) +
   143	        # intron[100,200) + exon[200,280) on chr1, forward strand.
   144	        # CIGAR per read: 80M 100N 80M
   145	        reads = [
   146	            {"name": f"r{i}", "pos": 20, "reverse": False,
   147	             "cigar": [(0, 80), (3, 100), (0, 80)]}
   148	            for i in range(3)
   149	        ]
   150	        bam_path = _write_bam_with_spliced_reads(
   151	            str(tmp_path), "chr1", chrom_len=400, reads=reads
   152	        )
   153	
   154	        interval = GenomicInterval(chrom="chr1", start=0, end=400, strand="+")
   155	        result = discover_candidates(
   156	            interval=interval,
   157	            bam_path=bam_path,
   158	            gtf_reader=None,         # explicitly no GTF
   159	            genome_fasta=genome,
   160	        )
   161	
   162	        # All candidates must be novel and have non-empty sequences.
   163	        assert len(result.candidates) >= 1
   164	        for c in result.candidates:
   165	            assert c.source == "novel"
   166	            assert c.sequence, f"Empty sequence for novel candidate {c.candidate_id}"
   167	            # Spliced length = sum of exon lengths, never including intron bases.
   168	            assert "C" not in c.sequence, (
   169	                "Intron bases (C) leaked into spliced sequence: "
   170	                f"{c.sequence[:50]}..."
   171	            )
   172	            # Should be exactly 80 A's + 80 G's = 160 bases.
   173	            assert len(c.sequence) == 160
   174	            assert c.sequence == "A" * 80 + "G" * 80
   175	
   176	    def test_novel_candidate_negative_strand_rc(self, tmp_path):
   177	        """Reverse-strand reads must yield reverse-complemented spliced cDNA."""
   178	        genome = "A" * 100 + "C" * 100 + "G" * 100 + "T" * 100
   179	        reads = [
   180	            {"name": f"r{i}", "pos": 20, "reverse": True,
   181	             "cigar": [(0, 80), (3, 100), (0, 80)]}
   182	            for i in range(2)
   183	        ]
   184	        bam_path = _write_bam_with_spliced_reads(
   185	            str(tmp_path), "chr1", chrom_len=400, reads=reads
   186	        )
   187	
   188	        interval = GenomicInterval(chrom="chr1", start=0, end=400, strand="-")
   189	        result = discover_candidates(
   190	            interval=interval,
   191	            bam_path=bam_path,
   192	            gtf_reader=None,
   193	            genome_fasta=genome,
   194	        )
   195	
   196	        assert len(result.candidates) >= 1
   197	        for c in result.candidates:
   198	            assert c.source == "novel"
   199	            assert c.sequence
   200	            # Plus-strand spliced = AAAAA...GGGGG (80 A + 80 G).
   201	            # Reverse complement = CCCCC...TTTTT (80 C + 80 T).
   202	            assert c.sequence == "C" * 80 + "T" * 80

     1	"""Tests for intron chain extraction and read grouping."""

     3	import sys
     4	from pathlib import Path

    11	    extract_intron_chain,

    18	class TestExtractIntronChain:
    19	    """Tests for CIGAR -> intron chain extraction."""
    20	
    21	    def test_single_exon_no_introns(self):
    22	        """A simple match with no N ops should produce empty intron chain."""
    23	        # 100M = 100bp match
    24	        cigar = [(0, 100)]
    25	        chain = extract_intron_chain(cigar, reference_start=1000)
    26	        assert chain.introns == ()
    27	        assert chain.is_single_exon
    28	        assert chain.num_introns == 0
    29	
    30	    def test_single_intron(self):
    31	        """50M 500N 50M should produce one intron."""
    32	        cigar = [(0, 50), (3, 500), (0, 50)]
    33	        chain = extract_intron_chain(cigar, reference_start=1000)
    34	        assert chain.introns == ((1050, 1550),)
    35	        assert chain.num_introns == 1
    36	        assert not chain.is_single_exon
    37	
    38	    def test_two_introns(self):
    39	        """30M 200N 40M 300N 30M should produce two introns."""
    40	        cigar = [(0, 30), (3, 200), (0, 40), (3, 300), (0, 30)]
    41	        chain = extract_intron_chain(cigar, reference_start=500)
    42	        assert chain.introns == ((530, 730), (770, 1070))
    43	        assert chain.num_introns == 2
    44	
    45	    def test_soft_clips_ignored(self):
    46	        """Soft clips (op=4) don't consume reference."""
    47	        cigar = [(4, 10), (0, 50), (3, 100), (0, 50), (4, 5)]
    48	        chain = extract_intron_chain(cigar, reference_start=0)
    49	        assert chain.introns == ((50, 150),)
    50	
    51	    def test_insertions_ignored(self):
    52	        """Insertions (op=1) don't consume reference."""
    53	        cigar = [(0, 30), (1, 5), (0, 20), (3, 100), (0, 50)]
    54	        chain = extract_intron_chain(cigar, reference_start=0)
    55	        # M consumes 30 ref, I doesn't, M consumes 20 ref -> intron at 50
    56	        assert chain.introns == ((50, 150),)
    57	
    58	    def test_deletions_consume_reference(self):
    59	        """Deletions (op=2) consume reference."""
    60	        cigar = [(0, 30), (2, 10), (0, 20), (3, 100), (0, 50)]
    61	        chain = extract_intron_chain(cigar, reference_start=0)
    62	        # M=30, D=10, M=20 -> ref_pos = 60 at N start
    63	        assert chain.introns == ((60, 160),)
    64	
    65	    def test_hashable_for_grouping(self):
    66	        """IntronChain should be usable as dict key."""
    67	        chain1 = extract_intron_chain([(0, 50), (3, 100), (0, 50)], 0)
    68	        chain2 = extract_intron_chain([(0, 50), (3, 100), (0, 50)], 0)
    69	        chain3 = extract_intron_chain([(0, 50), (3, 200), (0, 50)], 0)
    70	
    71	        assert chain1 == chain2
    72	        assert chain1 != chain3
    73	        assert hash(chain1) == hash(chain2)
    74	
    75	        d = {chain1: "a", chain3: "b"}
    76	        assert d[chain2] == "a"
    77

    89	        assert chain.introns == ()

    91	    def test_two_exon_transcript(self):
    92	        """Two exons with gap = one intron."""
    93	
    94	        class MockTranscript:
    95	            exons = [(100, 300), (500, 700)]
    96	
    97	        chain = gtf_transcript_to_intron_chain(MockTranscript())
    98	        assert chain.introns == ((300, 500),)
    99	
   100	    def test_three_exon_transcript(self):
   101	        """Three exons = two introns."""
   102	
   103	        class MockTranscript:
   104	            exons = [(100, 200), (400, 500), (700, 800)]
   105	
   106	        chain = gtf_transcript_to_intron_chain(MockTranscript())
   107	        assert chain.introns == ((200, 400), (500, 700))
   108	
   109	    def test_unsorted_exons(self):
   110	        """Exons should be sorted before computing gaps."""
   111	
   112	        class MockTranscript:
   113	            exons = [(500, 700), (100, 300)]
   114	
   115	        chain = gtf_transcript_to_intron_chain(MockTranscript())
   116	        assert chain.introns == ((300, 500),)

   119	class TestGroupReadsByThreePrimeAndIntronChain:
   120	    """Tests for read grouping."""
   121	
   122	    def _make_read(self, name, ref_start, cigar, ref_end, is_forward=True):
   123	        return {
   124	            "query_name": name,
   125	            "reference_start": ref_start,
   126	            "reference_end": ref_end,
   127	            "cigartuples": cigar,
   128	            "is_forward": is_forward,
   129	            "query_length": ref_end - ref_start,
   130	        }
   131	
   132	    def test_same_chain_close_three_prime(self):
   133	        """Reads with same intron chain and close 3' should group together."""
   134	        reads = [
   135	            self._make_read("r1", 100, [(0, 50), (3, 100), (0, 50)], 300),
   136	            self._make_read("r2", 100, [(0, 50), (3, 100), (0, 50)], 305),
   137	        ]
   138	        groups = group_reads_by_three_prime_and_intron_chain(reads, "+", threshold=24)
   139	        assert len(groups) == 1
   140	        key = list(groups.keys())[0]
   141	        assert len(groups[key]) == 2
   142	
   143	    def test_different_chains_separate_groups(self):
   144	        """Reads with different intron chains should be in separate groups."""
   145	        reads = [
   146	            self._make_read("r1", 100, [(0, 50), (3, 100), (0, 50)], 300),
   147	            self._make_read("r2", 100, [(0, 50), (3, 200), (0, 50)], 300),
   148	        ]
   149	        groups = group_reads_by_three_prime_and_intron_chain(reads, "+", threshold=24)
   150	        assert len(groups) == 2
   151	
   152	    def test_distant_three_prime_separate_groups(self):
   153	        """Reads with same chain but distant 3' should be in separate groups."""
   154	        reads = [
   155	            self._make_read("r1", 100, [(0, 100)], 200),
   156	            self._make_read("r2", 100, [(0, 100)], 300),
   157	        ]
   158	        groups = group_reads_by_three_prime_and_intron_chain(reads, "+", threshold=24)
   159	        assert len(groups) == 2
   160

   164	
   165	    def test_picks_longest(self):
   166	        reads = [

   168	            {"query_name": "long", "query_length": 500},
   169	            {"query_name": "medium", "query_length": 250},
   170	        ]
   171	        rep = pick_representative_read(reads)
   172	        assert rep["query_name"] == "long"

     1	"""Regression tests for fin/analysis/clustering.py bug fixes.
     2	
     3	Covers the novel-detection inner loop (formerly using a stale `ref_id` from
     4	the outer loop) in `ThreePrimePositionClustering.prepare_cluster_data`.
     5	"""
     6	
     7	from __future__ import annotations
     8	
     9	from fin.analysis.clustering import ThreePrimePositionClustering
    10	
    11	
    12	def _make_clusterer():
    13	    """Build a clusterer instance without opening BAM/FASTA."""
    14	    return ThreePrimePositionClustering.__new__(ThreePrimePositionClustering)
    15	
    16	
    17	def test_novel_detection_collapses_contained_read_plus_strand():
    18	    """A shorter novel read fully contained in a longer novel read should be
    19	    marked contained. Tests the post-fix inner loop variable scoping.
    20	    """
    21	    clusterer = _make_clusterer()
    22	
    23	    # Two novel reads on '+' strand: short read is a 3'-truncated version
    24	    # of long read (long extends 5 bp further at the 3' end).
    25	    read_seqs = {
    26	        "long_read": "ACGTACGTACGT",   # length 12
    27	        "short_read": "ACGTACG",       # length 7, prefix of long_read
    28	    }
    29	    ref_seqs = {}  # No GTF refs -> falls into novel detection loop
    30	
    31	    ids = ["long_read", "short_read"]
    32	    # 3' end positions on '+' strand: long_read extends past short_read
    33	    three_prime_positions = [112, 107]
    34	
    35	    result = clusterer.prepare_cluster_data(
    36	        ids=ids,
    37	        three_prime_positions=three_prime_positions,
    38	        read_seqs=read_seqs,
    39	        ref_seqs=ref_seqs,
    40	        strand="+",
    41	    )
    42	
    43	    # short_read is contained in long_read -> only long_read survives as ref
    44	    assert "long_read" in result.ref_seqs
    45	    assert "short_read" not in result.ref_seqs
    46	
    47	
    48	def test_novel_detection_keeps_distinct_reads():
    49	    """Two unrelated novel reads must both survive."""
    50	    clusterer = _make_clusterer()
    51	
    52	    read_seqs = {
    53	        "read_a": "AAAAAAAAAAAAA",
    54	        "read_b": "GGGGGGGGGGGGG",
    55	    }
    56	    ref_seqs = {}
    57	    ids = ["read_a", "read_b"]
    58	    three_prime_positions = [100, 100]
    59	
    60	    result = clusterer.prepare_cluster_data(
    61	        ids=ids,
    62	        three_prime_positions=three_prime_positions,
    63	        read_seqs=read_seqs,
    64	        ref_seqs=ref_seqs,
    65	        strand="+",
    66	    )
    67	
    68	    assert "read_a" in result.ref_seqs
    69	    assert "read_b" in result.ref_seqs
    70	
    71	
    72	def test_novel_detection_minus_strand():
    73	    """Same containment logic should hold on '-' strand with inverted end_dif."""
    74	    clusterer = _make_clusterer()
    75	
    76	    read_seqs = {
    77	        "long_read": "ACGTACGTACGT",
    78	        "short_read": "ACGTACG",
    79	    }
    80	    ref_seqs = {}
    81	    ids = ["long_read", "short_read"]
    82	    # On '-' strand, larger position = upstream; long extends further 3'
    83	    three_prime_positions = [100, 105]
    84	
    85	    result = clusterer.prepare_cluster_data(
    86	        ids=ids,
    87	        three_prime_positions=three_prime_positions,
    88	        read_seqs=read_seqs,
    89	        ref_seqs=ref_seqs,
    90	        strand="-",
    91	    )
    92	
    93	    assert "long_read" in result.ref_seqs
    94	    assert "short_read" not in result.ref_seqs

     1	"""
     2	GTF/GFF file format parser and writer
     3	"""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Generator, TYPE_CHECKING
     8	from pathlib import Path
     9	import logging
    10	
    11	if TYPE_CHECKING:
    12	    from fin.analysis.quantification import QuantResult
    13	
    14	
    15	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    16	
    17	
    18	class GTFTranscript:
    19	    """Represents a transcript with exons and optional CDS features"""
    20	
    21	    def __init__(self, transcript_id: str, gene_id: str, chrom: str,
    22	                 strand: str, source: str = 'unknown', score: Optional[float] = None,
    23	                 frame: Optional[int] = None, attributes: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None):
    24	        """
    25	        Initialize transcript
    26	
    27	        Args:
    28	            transcript_id: Transcript ID
    29	            gene_id: Gene ID
    30	            chrom: Chromosome name
    31	            strand: Strand (+ or -)
    32	            source: Source/annotation origin
    33	            score: Score value
    34	            frame: Frame information (0, 1, 2, or .)
    35	            attributes: Additional attributes
    36	        """
    37	        self.transcript_id = transcript_id
    38	        self.gene_id = gene_id
    39	        self.chrom = chrom
    40	        self.strand = strand
    41	        self.source = source
    42	        self.score = score
    43	        self.frame = frame
    44	        self.attributes = attributes or {}
    45	
    46	        self.exons = []  # List of (start, end) tuples
    47	        self.cds = []    # List of (start, end) tuples
    48	        self.start_codon = None
    49	        self.stop_codon = None
    50	        self.utr5 = []   # 5' UTR regions
    51	        self.utr3 = []   # 3' UTR regions
    52	
    53	    @property
    54	    def start(self) -> int:
    55	        """Get transcript start (minimum position of all features)"""
    56	        all_starts = []
    57	        if self.exons:
    58	            all_starts.extend([exon[0] for exon in self.exons])
    59	        if self.cds:
    60	            all_starts.extend([cds[0] for cds in self.cds])
    61	        return min(all_starts) if all_starts else 0
    62	
    63	    @property
    64	    def end(self) -> int:
    65	        """Get transcript end (maximum position of all features)"""
    66	        all_ends = []
    67	        if self.exons:
    68	            all_ends.extend([exon[1] for exon in self.exons])
    69	        if self.cds:
    70	            all_ends.extend([cds[1] for cds in self.cds])
    71	        return max(all_ends) if all_ends else 0
    72	
    73	    @property
    74	    def length(self) -> int:
    75	        """Get total transcript length including introns"""
    76	        return self.end - self.start
    77	
    78	    @property
    79	    def cds_length(self) -> int:
    80	        """Get total CDS length"""
    81	        return sum(end - start for start, end in self.cds)
    82	
    83	    @property
    84	    def exon_length(self) -> int:
    85	        """Get total exon length"""
    86	        return sum(end - start for start, end in self.exons)
    87	
    88	    @property
    89	    def num_exons(self) -> int:
    90	        """Get number of exons"""
    91	        return len(self.exons)
    92	
    93	    def sort_features(self):
    94	        """Sort exons and CDS by position"""
    95	        self.exons.sort()
    96	        self.cds.sort()
    97	
    98	    def add_exon(self, start: int, end: int):
    99	        """Add an exon"""
   100	        self.exons.append((start, end))
   101	
   102	    def add_cds(self, start: int, end: int):
   103	        """Add a CDS region"""
   104	        self.cds.append((start, end))
   105	
   106	    def get_spliced_sequence(self, fasta_seq: str) -> str:
   107	        """
   108	        Get spliced sequence for this transcript
   109	
   110	        Args:
   111	            fasta_seq: Full chromosome sequence as string
   112	
   113	        Returns:
   114	            Spliced transcript sequence
   115	        """
   116	        if not self.exons:
   117	            return ""
   118	
   119	        sequence = ""
   120	        for start, end in self.exons:
   121	            sequence += fasta_seq[start:end]
   122	
   123	        # Reverse complement for negative strand
   124	        if self.strand == '-':
   125	            sequence = self._reverse_complement(sequence)
   126	
   127	        return sequence
   128	
   129	    @staticmethod
   130	    def _reverse_complement(seq: str) -> str:
   131	        """Get reverse complement of a sequence"""
   132	        complement = {'A': 'T', 'T': 'A', 'C': 'G', 'G': 'C', 'N': 'N',
   133	                     'a': 't', 't': 'a', 'c': 'g', 'g': 'c', 'n': 'n'}
   134	        return "".join(complement.get(base, 'N') for base in reversed(seq))
   135	
   136	    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   137	        """Convert to dictionary"""
   138	        return {
   139	            'transcript_id': self.transcript_id,
   140	            'gene_id': self.gene_id,
   141	            'chrom': self.chrom,
   142	            'strand': self.strand,
   143	            'start': self.start,
   144	            'end': self.end,
   145	            'length': self.length,
   146	            'source': self.source,
   147	            'score': self.score,
   148	            'frame': self.frame,
   149	            'attributes': self.attributes,
   150	            'exons': self.exons,
   151	            'num_exons': self.num_exons,
   152	            'exon_length': self.exon_length,
   153	            'cds': self.cds,
   154	            'cds_length': self.cds_length,
   155	        }
   156	
   157	
   158	class GTFGene:
   159	    """Represents a gene with multiple transcripts"""
   160	
   161	    def __init__(self, gene_id: str, chrom: str, strand: str, source: str = 'unknown',
   162	                 score: Optional[float] = None, attributes: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None):
   163	        """
   164	        Initialize gene
   165	
   166	        Args:
   167	            gene_id: Gene ID
   168	            chrom: Chromosome name
   169	            strand: Strand (+ or -)
   170	            source: Source/annotation origin
   171	            score: Score value
   172	            attributes: Additional attributes
   173	        """
   174	        self.gene_id = gene_id
   175	        self.chrom = chrom
   176	        self.strand = strand
   177	        self.source = source
   178	        self.score = score
   179	        self.attributes = attributes or {}
   180	        self.transcripts = {}  # transcript_id -> GTFTranscript

[36m└[34m─([33m16:14:14[34m[00m on [32mdev[00m[36m ?✭[34m)[36m──> [?2004hexit[?2004l

[36m└[34m─([33m16:14:05[34m[00m[34m)[36m──> [?2004h(base) [36m┌─[30m([32m/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin[30m)[36m────────────────────────────[30m([36mlogan[30m@[32mmnemosyne4:pts/36[30m)[36m─┐
[36m└[34m─([33m16:14:05[34m[00m[36m ?✭[34m)[36m──> (base) [36m┌─[30m([32m/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin[30m)[36m────────────────────────────[30m([36mlogan[30m@[32mmnemosyne4:pts/36[30m)[36m─┐
[36m└[34m─([33m16:14:05[34m[00m on [32mdev[00m[36m ?✭[34m)[36m──> nl -ba fin/io/io_gtf.py | sed -n '240,330p'[?2004l
   240	    def __init__(self, file_path: str):
   241	        """
   242	        Initialize GTF/GFF reader
   243	
   244	        Args:
   245	            file_path: Path to GTF/GFF file
   246	        """
   247	        self.file_path = Path(file_path)
   248	        if not self.file_path.exists():
   249	            raise FileNotFoundError(f"GTF/GFF file not found: {file_path}")
   250	
   251	        self._file = None
   252	        self.genes = {}  # gene_id -> GTFGene
   253	        self.transcripts = {}  # transcript_id -> GTFTranscript
   254	        self._parsed = False
   255	
   256	    def __enter__(self):
   257	        """Context manager entry"""
   258	        self.open()
   259	        return self
   260	
   261	    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
   262	        """Context manager exit"""
   263	        self.close()
   264	
   265	    def open(self):
   266	        """Open GTF/GFF file"""
   267	        try:
   268	            self._file = open(self.file_path, 'r')
   269	            logger.info(f"Opened GTF/GFF file: {self.file_path}")
   270	        except Exception as e:
   271	            logger.error(f"Failed to open GTF/GFF file {self.file_path}: {e}")
   272	            raise
   273	
   274	    def close(self):
   275	        """Close file"""
   276	        if self._file is not None:
   277	            self._file.close()
   278	            self._file = None
   279	            logger.info(f"Closed GTF/GFF file: {self.file_path}")
   280	
   281	    def _parse_attributes(self, attr_str: str) -> Dict[str, str]:
   282	        """
   283	        Parse GTF/GFF attribute field
   284	
   285	        Args:
   286	            attr_str: Attribute string (key "value"; key2 "value2"; ...)
   287	
   288	        Returns:
   289	            Dictionary of attributes
   290	        """
   291	        if not attr_str or attr_str == '.':
   292	            return {}
   293	
   294	        attrs = {}
   295	
   296	        # Handle different formats (GTF with space separation, GFF with =)
   297	        if '; ' in attr_str or (attr_str.count(';') > 1 and '"' in attr_str):
   298	            # GTF format: key "value"; key2 "value2";
   299	            for attr in attr_str.rstrip(';').split('; '):
   300	                if not attr.strip():
   301	                    continue
   302	                parts = attr.strip().split(' ', 1)
   303	                if len(parts) == 2:
   304	                    key = parts[0].strip()
   305	                    value = parts[1].strip().strip('"')
   306	                    attrs[key] = value
   307	        else:
   308	            # GFF3 format: key=value; key2=value2
   309	            for attr in attr_str.rstrip(';').split(';'):
   310	                if not attr.strip():
   311	                    continue
   312	                if '=' in attr:
   313	                    key, value = attr.strip().split('=', 1)
   314	                    attrs[key.strip()] = value.strip().strip('"')
   315	                elif ' ' in attr:
   316	                    # Fallback to space-separated
   317	                    parts = attr.strip().split(' ', 1)
   318	                    if len(parts) == 2:
   319	                        key = parts[0].strip()
   320	                        value = parts[1].strip().strip('"')
   321	                        attrs[key] = value
   322	
   323	        return attrs
   324	
   325	    def _extract_id(self, attributes: Dict[str, str], id_keys: List[str]) -> Optional[str]:
   326	        """
   327	        Extract ID from attributes using multiple possible keys
   328	
   329	        Args:
   330	            attributes: Attribute dictionary
%                                                                               (base) [36m┌─[30m([32m/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin[30m)[36m────────────────────────────[30m([36mlogan[30m@[32mmnemosyne4:pts/36[30m)[36m─┐
[36m└[34m─([33m16:14:15[34m[00m on [32mdev[00m[36m ?✭[34m)[36m──> [?2004hnl -ba fin/io/io_gtf.py | sed -n '348,424p'[?2004l
   348	        self._file.seek(0)
   349	
   350	        # Common attribute keys to look for
   351	        gene_id_keys = ['gene_id', 'geneid', 'geneID', 'ID']
   352	        transcript_id_keys = ['transcript_id', 'transcriptid', 'transcriptID', 'Parent']
   353	
   354	        for line_num, line in enumerate(self._file, 1):
   355	            line = line.strip()
   356	            if not line or line.startswith('#'):
   357	                continue
   358	
   359	            try:
   360	                if line.startswith('#'):
   361	                    logger.warning('Comment line, skipped!')
   362	                # Parse GTF/GFF line
   363	                fields = line.split('\t')
   364	                if len(fields) < 9:
   365	                    logger.warning(f"Invalid GTF/GFF line at {line_num}: not enough fields")
   366	                    continue
   367	
   368	                chrom, source, feature_type, start_str, end_str, score_str, strand, frame_str, attr_str = fields[:9]
   369	
   370	                # Convert positions (GTF is 1-based, convert to 0-based)
   371	                start = int(start_str) - 1
   372	                end = int(end_str)
   373	                score = float(score_str) if score_str != '.' else None
   374	                frame = int(frame_str) if frame_str.isdigit() else None
   375	
   376	                # Parse attributes
   377	                attributes = self._parse_attributes(attr_str)
   378	
   379	                # Extract IDs
   380	                gene_id = self._extract_id(attributes, gene_id_keys)
   381	                transcript_id = self._extract_id(attributes, transcript_id_keys)
   382	
   383	                if not gene_id and not transcript_id:
   384	                    logger.warning(f"No gene or transcript ID found at line {line_num}")
   385	                    continue
   386	
   387	                # Use gene_id as transcript_id if only gene_id exists (simpler annotation)
   388	                if gene_id and not transcript_id:
   389	                    transcript_id = gene_id
   390	                elif transcript_id and not gene_id:
   391	                    # Try to find gene_id in attributes or use transcript_id as gene_id
   392	                    for key in ['gene_id', 'geneid', 'geneID', 'gene', 'ID']:
   393	                        gene_id = attributes.get(key, transcript_id)
   394	                        if gene_id:
   395	                            break
   396	
   397	                # Create/update gene
   398	                if gene_id not in self.genes:
   399	                    self.genes[gene_id] = GTFGene(
   400	                        gene_id=gene_id, chrom=chrom, strand=strand,
   401	                        source=source, score=score, attributes=attributes
   402	                    )
   403	
   404	                # Create/update transcript
   405	                if transcript_id not in self.transcripts:
   406	                    self.transcripts[transcript_id] = GTFTranscript(
   407	                        transcript_id=transcript_id, gene_id=gene_id, chrom=chrom,
   408	                        strand=strand, source=source, score=score, frame=frame,
   409	                        attributes=attributes
   410	                    )
   411	                    self.genes[gene_id].add_transcript(self.transcripts[transcript_id])
   412	
   413	                transcript_obj = self.transcripts[transcript_id]
   414	
   415	                # Add features based on feature_type
   416	                feature_type_lower = feature_type.lower()
   417	
   418	                if 'exon' in feature_type_lower:
   419	                    transcript_obj.add_exon(start, end)
   420	                elif 'cds' in feature_type_lower:
   421	                    transcript_obj.add_cds(start, end)
   422	                elif 'utr' in feature_type_lower:
   423	                    if '5' in feature_type_lower or 'five' in feature_type_lower:
   424	                        transcript_obj.utr5.append((start, end))

   493	    def get_transcripts_in_region(self, chrom: str, start: int, end: int) -> List[GTFTranscript]:
   494	        """
   495	        Get transcripts overlapping a region
   496	
   497	        Args:
   498	            chrom: Chromosome name
   499	            start: Start position (0-based)
   500	            end: End position
   501	
   502	        Returns:
   503	            List of GTFTranscript objects
   504	        """
   505	        txs_in_region = []
   506	        for tx in self.transcripts.values():
   507	            if tx.chrom == chrom and tx.start < end and tx.end > start:
   508	                txs_in_region.append(tx)
   509	        return txs_in_region

   525	    def _quant_cluster(
   526	        self,
   527	        candidate_set: CandidateSet,
   528	        read_ids: List[str],
   529	        interval: GenomicInterval,
   530	    ) -> List[QuantResult]:
   531	        """Within-cluster read assignment (quant_mode="cluster").
   532	
   533	        Each generation cluster (``CandidateSet.clusters``) is scored in isolation:
   534	        a read is aligned ONLY against that cluster's members (M1), straddling ties
   535	        are resolved by the summed-LLR junction signal (M2), and containment/
   536	        truncation ties fall to the cluster's main peak. Per-cluster assignment is
   537	        delegated to :func:`fin.pipeline.cluster_quant.assign_cluster_reads` (pure/
   538	        signal-agnostic); the M2 tiebreak is injected via ``m2_resolve_tie``. Cluster
   539	        results are folded back into per-candidate abundance/survival and emitted as
   540	        QuantResults (mirrors ``_quant_argmax_keep``).
   541	        """
   542	        import mappy
   543	
   544	        from fin.pipeline.cluster_quant import assign_cluster_reads
   545	        from fin.scoring.m2_junction_nll import m2_resolve_tie
   546	        from fin.scoring.mappy_preset import get_m1_preset
   547	        from fin.scoring.mappy_score import score_hit
   548	
   549	        cand_list = list(candidate_set.candidates)
   550	        read_sequences = getattr(candidate_set, "read_sequences", {}) or {}
   551	        n_c = len(cand_list)
   552	
   553	        # candidate_set.clusters holds candidate_ids per generation cluster. Translate
   554	        # to CURRENT list indices (ids dropped by post-discovery gates are skipped).
   555	        idx_of = {c.candidate_id: j for j, c in enumerate(cand_list)}
   556	        if candidate_set.clusters is None:
   557	            # Not chain-cluster mode: run unscoped over the whole candidate list.
   558	            logger.warning(
   559	                "quant_mode=cluster: interval %s has no cluster grouping "
   560	                "(clusters=None); treating all %d candidates as one cluster",
   561	                interval.region_string, n_c,
   562	            )
   563	            clusters = [list(range(n_c))]
   564	        else:
   565	            clusters = [
   566	                [idx_of[cid] for cid in ids if cid in idx_of]
   567	                for ids in candidate_set.clusters
   568	            ]
   569	            # Candidates added AFTER discovery (e.g. fusion augmentation) are in
   570	            # cand_list but not in any generation cluster; group them into ONE extra
%                                                                               (base) [36m┌─[30m([32m/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin[30m)[36m────────────────────────────[30m([36mlogan[30m@[32mmnemosyne4:pts/37[30m)[36m─┐

   136	    return abundance, weights

   139	def assign_cluster_reads(
   140	    members: Sequence[Chain],
   141	    as_rows: Dict[str, Dict[int, float]],
   142	    spans: Dict[str, Tuple[int, int]],
   143	    *,
   144	    m2_resolve: Optional[M2Resolver] = None,
   145	    min_support: float = 1.0,
   146	    m1_tie_margin: float = 1e-9,
   147	    em_max_iter: int = 200,
   148	    em_tol: float = 1e-9,
   149	) -> ClusterAssignment:
   150	    """Assign one cluster's reads to its members (M3 compat matrix -> EM) + survivors.
   151	
   152	    Args:
   153	        members: member intron chains (index == member id used everywhere else).
   154	        as_rows: read_id -> {member_idx: mappy AS} for members THIS read aligned to
   155	            (already restricted to this cluster's members by the caller).
   156	        spans: read_id -> (genomic_start, genomic_end) of the read's alignment.
   157	        m2_resolve: callback for straddling ties; returns (winner_member_idx, margin)
   158	            or None to abstain. A STRICT winner (margin > 0) makes the read certain;
   159	            an NLL tie / abstain leaves it ambiguous. When None, straddling ties stay
   160	            ambiguous.
   161	        min_support: a member survives iff its EM abundance >= this.
   162	        m1_tie_margin: AS margin for the best-AS tie set -- a read is unique-best only
   163	            when its top member beats the runner-up by MORE than this many AS points;
   164	            within the margin the members are TIED (go to M2 / ambiguous 1/k). A 2-3 bp
   165	            wobble shifts AS by only ~<=20, so a margin here stops a near-tie read from
   166	            spuriously anchoring a wobble shadow (measured: wobble AS-gaps concentrate
   167	            <=20, genuinely-different candidates differ by hundreds).
   168	        em_max_iter / em_tol: EM iteration cap / L1-convergence tolerance.
   169	
   170	    Returns:
   171	        ClusterAssignment with per-read EM responsibilities and the surviving members.
   172	    """
   173	    res = ClusterAssignment()
   174	    n = len(members)
   175	
   176	    # --- Build the M3 compatibility sets (which members each read can come from). ---
   177	    compat: Dict[str, List[int]] = {}
   178	    for rid, row in as_rows.items():
   179	        if not row:
   180	            continue
   181	        best = max(row.values())
   182	        if best <= 0:
   183	            continue
   184	        tie = sorted(j for j, v in row.items() if v >= best - m1_tie_margin)
   185	        if len(tie) == 1:                        # M1 certain
   186	            compat[rid] = [tie[0]]
   187	            res.n_unique += 1
   188	            continue
   189	        span = spans.get(rid)
   190	        straddles = span is not None and _straddles_difference(span, members, tie)
%                                                                               (base) [36m┌─[30m([32m/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin[30m)[36m────────────────────────────[30m([36mlogan[30m@[32mmnemosyne4:pts/38[30m)[36m─┐
[36m└[34m─([33m16:16:10[34m[00m on [32mdev[00m[36m ?✭[34m)[36m──> [?2004hexit[?2004l

    80	    def _record_drops(cols, reason: str) -> None:
    81	        for j in cols:
    82	            outcomes.append(SelectionOutcome(
    83	                cand_list[j].candidate_id, "drop", reason,
    84	            ))
    85	
    86	    # --- Cluster-internal wobble recheck (post-EM, data-layer shadow kill).
    87	    #     Cluster ALL multi-exon candidates by structure (same intron count +
    88	    #     every junction within +-bp); within a cluster the highest-EM-abundance
    89	    #     candidate anchors (often the true isoform, frequently a GTF
    90	    #     passthrough), and a NOVEL sibling whose abundance/anchor < fraction is
    91	    #     a wobble shadow -> drop. GTF/fusion never dropped (they only anchor).
    92	    #     Pure abundance evidence: GTF participates in the abundance race but is
    93	    #     NOT used as a correctness oracle, so this stays robust when the
    94	    #     annotation is wrong (and self-disables with no GTF: a real novel
    95	    #     anchors its own cluster). OFF -> no drops (byte-identical). ---
    96	    # Strand-keyed {strand: Counter{(donor,acceptor): n_reads}} of directly-
    97	    # observed read junctions; built lazily (reopens BAM) and shared by the
    98	    # cluster-recheck GTF guard and the Lever-2 junction-support gate.
    99	    observed_jct = None
   100	    if getattr(config, "m2_cluster_recheck", False):
   101	        from fin.scoring.m2_junction_nll import structural_wobble_clusters
   102	
   103	        frac = config.m2_cluster_recheck_fraction
   104	        if frac <= 0.0:
   105	            frac = config.min_isoform_fraction
   106	        if frac > 0.0:
   107	            em_ab = np.asarray(R).sum(axis=0)
   108	            bp = config.m2_cluster_recheck_bp
   109	            cassette_bp = getattr(

   111	            )
   112	            clusters = structural_wobble_clusters(
   113	                cand_list, bp, cassette_max_exon_bp=cassette_bp
   114	            )
   115	            from fin.scoring.m2_junction_nll import (
   116	                gtf_guard_needed,
   117	                wobble_shadow_drops,
   118	            )
   119	
   120	            gtf_drop = bool(getattr(
   121	                config, "m2_cluster_recheck_novel_displaces_gtf", True
   122	            ))
   123	            # Build the directly-observed read junctions for the GTF read-support
   124	            # guard ONLY when a clustered low-abundance GTF sibling could actually
   125	            # be dropped — this reopens the BAM, so skip it for no-GTF intervals
   126	            # (and avoids touching the BAM for mocked/placeholder-path callers).
   127	            if gtf_drop and gtf_guard_needed(cand_list, em_ab, clusters, frac):
   128	                observed_jct = observed_junctions_fn(interval)
   129	            _d = wobble_shadow_drops(

   132	                observed_jct=observed_jct,
   133	                jct_tol=int(getattr(config, "m2_cluster_recheck_jct_tol", 2)),
   134	                gtf_min_jct_reads=int(getattr(
   135	                    config, "m2_cluster_recheck_gtf_min_jct_reads", 1
   136	                )),
   137	            )
   138	            drop_cols |= _d
   139	            _record_drops(_d, "cluster_recheck")
