     1	"""De-novo intron graph: assemble full-length transcript intron chains from
     2	partial/truncated read chains.
     3	
     4	Measured motivation (p00, no annotation): pyfin's dominant de-novo error is
     5	truncation — candidates that are a contiguous sub-chain of a real transcript
     6	(gffcompare class 'c', ~16% of output), because dRNA reads are 3'-biased so a
     7	read covers only the 3' junctions. pyfin currently emits each read's exact chain
     8	as a candidate, so a truncated read -> a truncated candidate.
     9	
    10	This module pools all reads' junctions, clusters wobbled junctions to a
    11	read-count consensus, builds a directed read-adjacency graph (nodes = consensus
    12	junctions ordered by genomic coord; edges = "these two junctions are consecutive
    13	in >= min_edge_reads reads"), and EXTENDS each read's chain through
    14	UNAMBIGUOUSLY-supported edges to a maximal chain. Extension stops at genuine
    15	branch points (a node with >1 supported successor), so it lengthens truncated
    16	chains WITHOUT fabricating wrong junction combinations.
    17	
    18	NOT annotation-snap: consensus coordinates and edges come only from reads.
    19	"""
    20	from __future__ import annotations
    21	
    22	from collections import Counter, defaultdict
    23	from typing import Dict, List, Set, Tuple
    24	
    25	Junction = Tuple[int, int]
    26	Chain = Tuple[Junction, ...]
    27	
    28	
    29	def cluster_junctions(chains: List[Chain], tol: int) -> Dict[Junction, Junction]:
    30	    """Map every observed junction to a consensus junction. Greedy by read count:
    31	    the most-observed junction seeds a centroid; a junction within ``tol`` bp on
    32	    BOTH donor and acceptor maps to the first matching centroid. Deterministic
    33	    (count desc, then coordinate)."""
    34	    counts: Counter = Counter()
    35	    for ch in chains:
    36	        for j in ch:
    37	            counts[j] += 1
    38	    consensus: Dict[Junction, Junction] = {}
    39	    centroids: List[Junction] = []
    40	    for j in sorted(counts, key=lambda x: (-counts[x], x)):
    41	        hit = None
    42	        for cj in centroids:
    43	            if abs(j[0] - cj[0]) <= tol and abs(j[1] - cj[1]) <= tol:
    44	                hit = cj
    45	                break
    46	        if hit is None:
    47	            centroids.append(j)
    48	            consensus[j] = j
    49	        else:
    50	            consensus[j] = hit
    51	    return consensus
    52	
    53	
    54	def build_graph(cons_chains: List[Chain]) -> Tuple[Counter, Counter]:
    55	    """Node + edge read-support counts from consensus-mapped chains.
    56	    Edge (a, b) = a and b are consecutive junctions in a read's chain."""
    57	    node_counts: Counter = Counter()
    58	    edge_counts: Counter = Counter()
    59	    for ch in cons_chains:
    60	        for j in ch:
    61	            node_counts[j] += 1
    62	        for a, b in zip(ch, ch[1:]):
    63	            edge_counts[(a, b)] += 1
    64	    return node_counts, edge_counts
    65	
    66	
    67	def anchored_5p_junctions(
    68	    cons_chains: List[Chain],
    69	    five_prime_pos: List[int],
    70	    strand: str,
    71	    tss_tol: int,
    72	    min_tss_reads: int,
    73	    tss_frac: float,
    74	) -> Set[Junction]:
    75	    """Return the set of 5'-terminal consensus junctions that sit at a real TSS.
    76	
    77	    dRNA is 3'-biased: a truncated read of a LONG transcript stops at a RANDOM 5'
    78	    position, but a COMPLETE read of a genuine short isoform stops at that
    79	    isoform's real TSS. So among the reads whose 5'-most junction is ``J`` (they
    80	    begin at ``J``), if a strong fraction pile their read-5'-ends within
    81	    ``tss_tol`` bp of one position, ``J`` marks a real transcription start and a
    82	    chain ending there must NOT be extended further 5'-ward (that would erase the
    83	    short isoform). Empirically (p00) such peaks carry 40-90% of a locus's reads;
    84	    degradation-only starts scatter and never cross ``tss_frac``.
    85	
    86	    5'-most junction is ``chain[0]`` on '+' (5' = small coord) and ``chain[-1]``
    87	    on '-' (5' = large coord).
    88	    """
    89	    by_term: Dict[Junction, List[int]] = defaultdict(list)
    90	    for cc, p in zip(cons_chains, five_prime_pos):
    91	        if not cc or p is None:
    92	            continue
    93	        term = cc[0] if strand == "+" else cc[-1]
    94	        by_term[term].append(p)
    95	    anchored: Set[Junction] = set()
    96	    for term, positions in by_term.items():
    97	        if len(positions) < min_tss_reads:
    98	            continue
    99	        best = max(sum(1 for q in positions if abs(q - p) <= tss_tol)
   100	                   for p in positions)
   101	        if best >= min_tss_reads and best >= tss_frac * len(positions):
   102	            anchored.add(term)
   103	    return anchored
   104	
   105	
   106	def assemble_chains(
   107	    chains: List[Chain],
   108	    tol: int,
   109	    min_edge_reads: int,
   110	    five_prime_pos: List[int] = None,
   111	    strand: str = "+",
   112	    tss_tol: int = 20,
   113	    min_tss_reads: int = 3,
   114	    tss_frac: float = 0.4,
   115	) -> Tuple[List[Chain], Dict[int, Chain]]:
   116	    """Assemble maximal chains by unambiguous extension.
   117	
   118	    Returns ``(cons_chains, extended_by_index)`` where ``cons_chains[i]`` is input
   119	    chain ``i`` mapped to consensus junctions, and ``extended_by_index[i]`` is the
   120	    maximal chain that chain ``i`` extends into (following only edges from a node
   121	    that has exactly ONE supported successor / predecessor). Two input reads whose
   122	    (possibly truncated) chains lie on the same maximal path map to the SAME
   123	    extended chain, so downstream grouping by the extended chain merges them.
   124	
   125	    5'-TSS brake: if ``five_prime_pos`` (per-input-chain read-5'-end genomic
   126	    positions) is given, a chain whose 5'-terminal junction is a real TSS
   127	    (:func:`anchored_5p_junctions`) is NOT extended in the 5' direction, so
   128	    genuine short isoforms contained inside a longer transcript survive instead of
   129	    being merged away. Without ``five_prime_pos`` behaviour is byte-identical to
   130	    the un-braked assembly (default-off contract).
   131	    """
   132	    cons = cluster_junctions(chains, tol)
   133	    cons_chains: List[Chain] = [tuple(cons[j] for j in ch) for ch in chains]
   134	    _, edge_counts = build_graph(cons_chains)
   135	
   136	    succ: Dict[Junction, List[Junction]] = defaultdict(list)
   137	    pred: Dict[Junction, List[Junction]] = defaultdict(list)
   138	    for (a, b), n in edge_counts.items():
   139	        if n >= min_edge_reads:
   140	            succ[a].append(b)
   141	            pred[b].append(a)
   142	
   143	    anchored: Set[Junction] = set()
   144	    if five_prime_pos is not None:
   145	        anchored = anchored_5p_junctions(
   146	            cons_chains, five_prime_pos, strand, tss_tol, min_tss_reads, tss_frac
   147	        )
   148	
   149	    def unique_succ(j: Junction):
   150	        s = succ.get(j)
   151	        return s[0] if s and len(s) == 1 else None
   152	
   153	    def unique_pred(j: Junction):
   154	        p = pred.get(j)
   155	        return p[0] if p and len(p) == 1 else None
   156	
   157	    cache: Dict[Chain, Chain] = {}
   158	
   159	    def extend(cc: Chain) -> Chain:
   160	        if cc in cache:
   161	            return cache[cc]
   162	        if not cc:
   163	            cache[cc] = cc
   164	            return cc
   165	        chain = list(cc)
   166	        seen: Set[Junction] = set(chain)
   167	        # 5' terminus of THIS read's chain (fixed; 3'-ward extension won't move it)
   168	        five_term = cc[0] if strand == "+" else cc[-1]
   169	        braked = five_term in anchored
   170	        # right extension is 5'-ward on '-' strand, 3'-ward on '+'
   171	        block_right = braked and strand == "-"
   172	        # left extension is 5'-ward on '+' strand, 3'-ward on '-'
   173	        block_left = braked and strand == "+"
   174	        while not block_right:
   175	            nxt = unique_succ(chain[-1])
   176	            if nxt is None or nxt in seen:
   177	                break
   178	            chain.append(nxt)
   179	            seen.add(nxt)
   180	        while not block_left:
%                                                                               (base) [36m┌─[30m([32m/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin[30m)[36m────────────────────────────[30m([36mlogan[30m@[32mmnemosyne4:pts/40[30m)[36m─┐
[36m└[34m─([33m16:18:17[34m[00m on [32mdev[00m[36m ?✭[34m)[36m──> [?2004hnl -ba fin/candidates/junction_graph.py | sed -n '180<dates/junction_graph.py | sed -n '180,                                       <dates/junction_graph.py | sed -n '180,196p'[?2004l
   180	        while not block_left:
   181	            prv = unique_pred(chain[0])
   182	            if prv is None or prv in seen:
   183	                break
   184	            chain.insert(0, prv)
   185	            seen.add(prv)
   186	        out = tuple(chain)
   187	        cache[cc] = out
   188	        return out
   189	
   190	    extended_by_index: Dict[int, Chain] = {
   191	        i: extend(cc) for i, cc in enumerate(cons_chains)
   192	    }
   193	    return cons_chains, extended_by_index
%                                                                               (base) [36m┌─[30m([32m/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin[30m)[36m────────────────────────────[30m([36mlogan[30m@[32mmnemosyne4:pts/40[30m)[36m─┐
[36m└[34m─([33m16:18:27[34m[00m on [32mdev[00m[36m ?✭[34m)[36m──> [?2004hexit[?2004l

  ✓ Read discovery, chain clustering, intron-chain, dataclass, and config code
  ✓ Trace annotated and de-novo candidate paths with defaults
  ✓ Assess correctness and wobble-shadow behavior
  ✓ Identify tuning targets and risks
codex
**Bottom Line**

Production Phase 1 is the chain-cluster path, not the legacy 3-prime plus exact-chain path: `PipelineConfig.chain_cluster_discovery=True` and the runner passes it into `discover_candidates` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:36), [runner.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/runner.py:171)). Directly calling `discover_candidates()` without config is different: its function default is `chain_cluster=False` ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:351)).

The suspected p00 root cause is confirmed with nuance: chain-cluster does recognize wobble variants and puts them in the same generation cluster, but it keeps each wobbled chain as a separate candidate. So `(1000,2000)` and `(1002,1998)` do not remain invisible to each other, but both still survive as distinct `TranscriptCandidate`s unless later filters remove one.

**1. Mechanism**

`discover_candidates` returns a `CandidateSet`: `interval`, `candidates`, `read_ids`, `read_sequences`, and, only for chain-cluster mode, `clusters`, `shadows`, and `read_spans` ([dataclasses.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/dataclasses.py:75)). Each candidate has `candidate_id`, exact `IntronChain`, `three_prime_pos`, `sequence`, `source`, `supporting_read_ids`, `chrom`, `strand`, `start`, `end` ([dataclasses.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/dataclasses.py:29)). `IntronChain` is a frozen dataclass over a tuple of `(start,end)` introns, so equality/hash are exact-coordinate equality ([dataclasses.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/dataclasses.py:11)).

Input reads are fetched from BAM, unmapped reads are skipped, interval strand is enforced when present, `all_read_ids` is filled, primary query sequences are captured, then fusion-like reads are excluded from non-fusion discovery ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:386)). BAM dicts include `query_name`, flags, CIGAR, `reference_start`, `reference_end`, `is_forward`, `query_length`, and `query_sequence` ([io_bam.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/io/io_bam.py:216)).

GTF passthrough: if there are no non-fusion reads, discovery returns no candidates before loading GTF ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:417)). Otherwise, GTF transcripts overlapping the interval are fetched by chromosome and span overlap ([io_gtf.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/io/io_gtf.py:493)). Opposite-strand annotation is skipped in stranded intervals ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:221), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:491)). A transcript becomes `TranscriptCandidate(candidate_id=tx.transcript_id, source="gtf")`; its chain is computed from sorted exon gaps, 3-prime is `tx.end` on `+` and `tx.start` on `-`, sequence is `tx.get_spliced_sequence(genome_fasta)`, and support starts empty ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:230), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:233), [intron_chains.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/intron_chains.py:46)). GTF parsing converts GTF starts from 1-based to 0-based while keeping end as half-open-style exclusive ([io_gtf.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/io/io_gtf.py:370)).

Novel default path: chain-cluster. Reads are converted to CIGAR-derived chains with exact half-open introns: each `N` operation records `(ref_pos, ref_pos + length)`, while `M/D/=/X` and `N` consume reference; `S/I/H/P` do not ([intron_chains.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/intron_chains.py:18)). `_chain_cluster_candidates` records `(read, chain)` plus `read_spans[qname]=(reference_start, reference_end)` ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:201)).

`cluster_read_chains` first groups by exact `tuple(chain.introns)` and read ID set, explicitly ignoring 3-prime ends ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:201)). Exact contiguous subchains fold into the longest exact container; with the default span guard, reads that exonically span a container’s extra intron are kept as their own candidate instead of folded ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:220)). Single-exon reads can be folded into a multi-exon candidate only if their span lies inside one exon and overlaps no intron ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:149), [chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:264)).

Surviving multi-exon chains are union-find clustered by: same intron count with all junction ends within `wobble_bp`; cassette skip with one small skipped exon; or tolerant containment ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:93), [chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:113), [chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:136), [chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:260)). Crucially, those clusters keep all member chains; no coordinates are snapped or merged ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:17)).

Emission: `_chain_cluster_candidates` iterates every cluster member. Multi-exon members are matched to GTF by exact chain only; otherwise each member emits one novel candidate with UUID-like `novel_xxxxxxxx`, source `"novel"`, support from that member’s reads, span as union of those reads’ spans, and sequence stitched from genome exons ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:256), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:273)). Mono members are split into spatial overlap clusters before emission, so all empty-chain reads do not become one giant single-exon candidate ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:37), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:294)). Low-support novel filtering happens after emission when `min_novel_reads > 1`; default is 1, so no default drop ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:308)).

Legacy path exists only when chain-cluster is off. It groups by `(consensus_3prime, exact IntronChain)`, where 3-prime is `reference_end` for forward reads and `reference_start` for reverse reads ([intron_chains.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/intron_chains.py:486), [interval_manager.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/io/interval_manager.py:191)). It then collapses novel candidates by exact chain plus nearby 3-prime; optional wobble collapse runs only if `denovo_wobble_tol > 0` ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:588), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:794)).

**2. Defaults**

From `PipelineConfig`:

| Feature | Default |
|---|---:|
| `three_prime_threshold` | `24` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:26)) |
| `min_novel_reads` | `1` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:28)) |
| `chain_cluster_discovery` | `True` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:36)) |
| `chain_cluster_wobble_bp` | `6` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:37)) |
| `chain_cluster_cassette_max_exon_bp` | `70` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:38)) |
| `chain_cluster_fold_monoexon` | `True` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:39)) |
| `chain_cluster_fold_span_guard` | `True` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:40)) |
| `denovo_wobble_tol` | `0`, off ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:394)) |
| `denovo_wobble_shadow_ratio` | `0.5` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:403)) |
| `denovo_graph` | `False` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:415)) |
| `denovo_graph_tol` | `6` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:416)) |
| `denovo_graph_min_edge_reads` | `2` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:417)) |
| `denovo_graph_tss_brake` | `True` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:426)) |
| `denovo_graph_tss_tol` | `20` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:427)) |
| `denovo_graph_tss_min_reads` | `3` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:428)) |
| `denovo_graph_tss_frac` | `0.4` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:429)) |

Important: `denovo_graph` and `denovo_wobble_tol` are bypassed when `chain_cluster=True`, because `discover_candidates` returns from `_chain_cluster_candidates` before reaching the legacy graph/wobble code ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:428), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:467)).

**3. Soundness Notes**

The coordinate model looks internally consistent: CIGAR `N` extraction is 0-based half-open, and GTF parsing converts to the same convention before exon-gap intron construction ([intron_chains.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/intron_chains.py:35), [io_gtf.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/io/io_gtf.py:370)).

The major generation-level correctness issue for de-novo precision is that wobble clustering is metadata, not collapse. `ChainCluster.members` remain distinct candidates, and `_chain_cluster_candidates` emits each member separately ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:319), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:275)).

Other edge cases worth noting:

- Default chain-cluster ignores 3-prime clustering entirely. Same-chain reads with distant TES/TSS are pooled into one member, and candidate `three_prime_pos` becomes union-span end on `+` or start on `-` ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:201), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:268)). This can collapse same-chain terminal isoforms.
- Multi-exon GTF absorption in chain-cluster is exact-chain only, with no 3-prime threshold; same-chain but terminally different read groups can be absorbed into the first matching GTF candidate ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:239), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:276)).
- Secondary alignments are not filtered before candidate generation. The same `query_name` can support multiple chains if multiple alignments overlap; `read_spans[qname]` can also be overwritten by later alignments ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:390), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:209)).
- `all_read_ids` is populated before fusion filtering, so fusion/supplementary IDs can remain in `CandidateSet.read_ids` even though their reads did not contribute to non-fusion candidates ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:403), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:413)).
- Novel IDs are UUID-derived, so candidate IDs are intentionally nondeterministic across runs ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:25)).
- If `interval.strand is None`, discovery defaults candidate strand to `"+"`; correct behavior depends on the interval generator always being strand-separated ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:399), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:425)).

**4. Wobble Root Cause**

Confirmed for candidate survival.

For two reads with chains `(1000,2000)` and `(1002,1998)`:

1. They first become different exact keys because `by_chain` uses `tuple(chain.introns)` ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:201)).
2. `_wobble` returns true if same intron count and both boundaries are within `wobble_bp` ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:93)).
3. `_related` puts them in one union-find component ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:136)).
4. But the component becomes multiple `GenCandidate` members, not one consensus candidate ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:320)).
5. `_chain_cluster_candidates` emits one `TranscriptCandidate` per member ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:273)).

The tests state this intended behavior directly: “2bp-wobbled sibling: NOT an exact subchain -> both KEPT, one cluster” ([test_chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/tests/unit/test_chain_cluster.py:81)).

So the precise survival point is `ChainCluster.members -> for m in cl.members -> _emit(...)`. Wobble/cassette/containment clustering merges candidates only into a downstream cluster, not into one generated candidate.

**5. Tuning Target**

Highest-leverage Phase 1 change: add generation-time wobble absorption/consensus inside the chain-cluster path before `TranscriptCandidate` emission. Increasing `chain_cluster_wobble_bp` alone will not improve generated structural precision, because it only broadens cluster membership while still keeping every member candidate.

A conservative target would be: within each same-intron-count wobble family, choose a read-derived representative or consensus, union the shadow reads into it, and emit one candidate only when the lower-support sibling looks like a shadow. The existing legacy `_wobble_collapse_novel` already encodes the right philosophy: merge wobbled novel candidates into the highest-read-support representative before they exist as separate candidates, with a support-ratio guard to protect real close isoforms ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:666)). But that code is currently unreachable in the production chain-cluster path.

Risks: real close donor/acceptor isoforms, NAGNAG-like events, and low-abundance true isoforms can be over-merged. Avoid extending this collapse to cassette and containment by default; those are much more biologically plausible. Also avoid solving this purely post-hoc: once shadows are emitted and quantified, dropping or merging them reshuffles read mass. The generation-time merge avoids that read-reassignment trap because reads never compete through a separate shadow candidate in the first place ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:684)).

I did not modify files or run the test suite.
tokens used
156,152
**Bottom Line**

Production Phase 1 is the chain-cluster path, not the legacy 3-prime plus exact-chain path: `PipelineConfig.chain_cluster_discovery=True` and the runner passes it into `discover_candidates` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:36), [runner.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/runner.py:171)). Directly calling `discover_candidates()` without config is different: its function default is `chain_cluster=False` ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:351)).

The suspected p00 root cause is confirmed with nuance: chain-cluster does recognize wobble variants and puts them in the same generation cluster, but it keeps each wobbled chain as a separate candidate. So `(1000,2000)` and `(1002,1998)` do not remain invisible to each other, but both still survive as distinct `TranscriptCandidate`s unless later filters remove one.

**1. Mechanism**

`discover_candidates` returns a `CandidateSet`: `interval`, `candidates`, `read_ids`, `read_sequences`, and, only for chain-cluster mode, `clusters`, `shadows`, and `read_spans` ([dataclasses.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/dataclasses.py:75)). Each candidate has `candidate_id`, exact `IntronChain`, `three_prime_pos`, `sequence`, `source`, `supporting_read_ids`, `chrom`, `strand`, `start`, `end` ([dataclasses.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/dataclasses.py:29)). `IntronChain` is a frozen dataclass over a tuple of `(start,end)` introns, so equality/hash are exact-coordinate equality ([dataclasses.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/dataclasses.py:11)).

Input reads are fetched from BAM, unmapped reads are skipped, interval strand is enforced when present, `all_read_ids` is filled, primary query sequences are captured, then fusion-like reads are excluded from non-fusion discovery ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:386)). BAM dicts include `query_name`, flags, CIGAR, `reference_start`, `reference_end`, `is_forward`, `query_length`, and `query_sequence` ([io_bam.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/io/io_bam.py:216)).

GTF passthrough: if there are no non-fusion reads, discovery returns no candidates before loading GTF ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:417)). Otherwise, GTF transcripts overlapping the interval are fetched by chromosome and span overlap ([io_gtf.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/io/io_gtf.py:493)). Opposite-strand annotation is skipped in stranded intervals ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:221), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:491)). A transcript becomes `TranscriptCandidate(candidate_id=tx.transcript_id, source="gtf")`; its chain is computed from sorted exon gaps, 3-prime is `tx.end` on `+` and `tx.start` on `-`, sequence is `tx.get_spliced_sequence(genome_fasta)`, and support starts empty ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:230), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:233), [intron_chains.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/intron_chains.py:46)). GTF parsing converts GTF starts from 1-based to 0-based while keeping end as half-open-style exclusive ([io_gtf.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/io/io_gtf.py:370)).

Novel default path: chain-cluster. Reads are converted to CIGAR-derived chains with exact half-open introns: each `N` operation records `(ref_pos, ref_pos + length)`, while `M/D/=/X` and `N` consume reference; `S/I/H/P` do not ([intron_chains.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/intron_chains.py:18)). `_chain_cluster_candidates` records `(read, chain)` plus `read_spans[qname]=(reference_start, reference_end)` ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:201)).

`cluster_read_chains` first groups by exact `tuple(chain.introns)` and read ID set, explicitly ignoring 3-prime ends ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:201)). Exact contiguous subchains fold into the longest exact container; with the default span guard, reads that exonically span a container’s extra intron are kept as their own candidate instead of folded ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:220)). Single-exon reads can be folded into a multi-exon candidate only if their span lies inside one exon and overlaps no intron ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:149), [chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:264)).

Surviving multi-exon chains are union-find clustered by: same intron count with all junction ends within `wobble_bp`; cassette skip with one small skipped exon; or tolerant containment ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:93), [chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:113), [chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:136), [chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:260)). Crucially, those clusters keep all member chains; no coordinates are snapped or merged ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:17)).

Emission: `_chain_cluster_candidates` iterates every cluster member. Multi-exon members are matched to GTF by exact chain only; otherwise each member emits one novel candidate with UUID-like `novel_xxxxxxxx`, source `"novel"`, support from that member’s reads, span as union of those reads’ spans, and sequence stitched from genome exons ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:256), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:273)). Mono members are split into spatial overlap clusters before emission, so all empty-chain reads do not become one giant single-exon candidate ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:37), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:294)). Low-support novel filtering happens after emission when `min_novel_reads > 1`; default is 1, so no default drop ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:308)).

Legacy path exists only when chain-cluster is off. It groups by `(consensus_3prime, exact IntronChain)`, where 3-prime is `reference_end` for forward reads and `reference_start` for reverse reads ([intron_chains.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/intron_chains.py:486), [interval_manager.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/io/interval_manager.py:191)). It then collapses novel candidates by exact chain plus nearby 3-prime; optional wobble collapse runs only if `denovo_wobble_tol > 0` ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:588), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:794)).

**2. Defaults**

From `PipelineConfig`:

| Feature | Default |
|---|---:|
| `three_prime_threshold` | `24` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:26)) |
| `min_novel_reads` | `1` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:28)) |
| `chain_cluster_discovery` | `True` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:36)) |
| `chain_cluster_wobble_bp` | `6` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:37)) |
| `chain_cluster_cassette_max_exon_bp` | `70` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:38)) |
| `chain_cluster_fold_monoexon` | `True` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:39)) |
| `chain_cluster_fold_span_guard` | `True` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:40)) |
| `denovo_wobble_tol` | `0`, off ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:394)) |
| `denovo_wobble_shadow_ratio` | `0.5` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:403)) |
| `denovo_graph` | `False` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:415)) |
| `denovo_graph_tol` | `6` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:416)) |
| `denovo_graph_min_edge_reads` | `2` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:417)) |
| `denovo_graph_tss_brake` | `True` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:426)) |
| `denovo_graph_tss_tol` | `20` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:427)) |
| `denovo_graph_tss_min_reads` | `3` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:428)) |
| `denovo_graph_tss_frac` | `0.4` ([config.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/pipeline/config.py:429)) |

Important: `denovo_graph` and `denovo_wobble_tol` are bypassed when `chain_cluster=True`, because `discover_candidates` returns from `_chain_cluster_candidates` before reaching the legacy graph/wobble code ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:428), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:467)).

**3. Soundness Notes**

The coordinate model looks internally consistent: CIGAR `N` extraction is 0-based half-open, and GTF parsing converts to the same convention before exon-gap intron construction ([intron_chains.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/intron_chains.py:35), [io_gtf.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/io/io_gtf.py:370)).

The major generation-level correctness issue for de-novo precision is that wobble clustering is metadata, not collapse. `ChainCluster.members` remain distinct candidates, and `_chain_cluster_candidates` emits each member separately ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:319), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:275)).

Other edge cases worth noting:

- Default chain-cluster ignores 3-prime clustering entirely. Same-chain reads with distant TES/TSS are pooled into one member, and candidate `three_prime_pos` becomes union-span end on `+` or start on `-` ([chain_cluster.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/chain_cluster.py:201), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:268)). This can collapse same-chain terminal isoforms.
- Multi-exon GTF absorption in chain-cluster is exact-chain only, with no 3-prime threshold; same-chain but terminally different read groups can be absorbed into the first matching GTF candidate ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:239), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:276)).
- Secondary alignments are not filtered before candidate generation. The same `query_name` can support multiple chains if multiple alignments overlap; `read_spans[qname]` can also be overwritten by later alignments ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:390), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:209)).
- `all_read_ids` is populated before fusion filtering, so fusion/supplementary IDs can remain in `CandidateSet.read_ids` even though their reads did not contribute to non-fusion candidates ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:403), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:413)).
- Novel IDs are UUID-derived, so candidate IDs are intentionally nondeterministic across runs ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:25)).
- If `interval.strand is None`, discovery defaults candidate strand to `"+"`; correct behavior depends on the interval generator always being strand-separated ([discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:399), [discovery.py](/SSD/logan/dev/pyfin/fin/candidates/discovery.py:425)).
